Life Protection & Saving API Documentation


Life Protection & Saving APIs
Get Eligible Products Get Investment Funds Create Quotations Create Policy Get All Policy for the User Retrieves questions description and codes Retrieves occupation description and codes Download Proposal Report Download Illustration Report Get Policy Number Download Policy Report Get debiting day Retrieve Customer Data Update Proposal or Quotation

Life Protection & Saving APIs

Protect and saving policies typically refer to various measures, strategies, or agreements designed to safeguard assets, resources, or individuals' interests, often within the context of finance, insurance, or risk management. These policies aim to mitigate risks, provide financial security, and promote responsible stewardship of resources.

Get Eligible Products

Endpoint: /products?

Swagger Link

Based on the user's age, this API will return eligible products applicable for the user and maximum policy tenure.

Request Example

                    
header
    Content-Type: application/json
    client_id: API Key
Query Params: 
    transactionId:%3Cstring%3E
    language:en
    dateOfBirthG:%3Cstring%3E   
    dateOfBirthH:%3Cstring%3E                
                    
                  
                    
curl --location --globoff '{{baseUrl}}/products?transactionId=%3Cstring%3E&language=en&dateOfBirthG=%3Cstring%3E&dateOfBirthH=%3Cstring%3E' \
--header 'Accept: application/json' \
--header 'client_id: '

Get Investment Funds

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/funds?

Swagger Link

Returns all the available funds where user's money will be invested.

Request Example

                      
header:
    authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb
    Accept:application/json
    Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e
Query Params: 
    transactionId:12345
    language:en
                      
                    
                        
curl --location 'https://apimarketplace-ext-sit-gw-svc-gw-gateway-traffic-cp4i-nprd.apimp-nprd-cl01-de8fb88b0db8c47d4745b3af8ac7158d-0000.eu-de.containers.appdomain.cloud/apimarketplace-sit/api-marketplace-sit//v1/penta/art/funds?transactionId=12345&language=en' \
--header 'Accept: application/json' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb' \

Create Policy

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/policy-create-receipt

Swagger Link

Creates a proposal deposit receipt and posts the accounting for it, receipt creation is needed before issuing policy, after this call apigee will call issue policy internally.

Create Policy Workflow

Click here


Request Example

                                    
header:
    authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb
    Accept:*/*
    Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e
    Content-Type: application/json
body:
    data {
        "policyNo": "IBER000300016567",
        "proposalNo": "API33313",
        "instrumentType": "DD",
        "instrumentBank": "ARB",
        "instrumentBankBranch": "CST ARB CUSTOMERS",
        "receiptCurrency": "SAR"
    }      
                                    
                                  
                                      
curl --location 'https://apimarketplace-ext-sit-gw-svc-gw-gateway-traffic-cp4i-nprd.apimp-nprd-cl01-de8fb88b0db8c47d4745b3af8ac7158d-0000.eu-de.containers.appdomain.cloud/apimarketplace-sit/api-marketplace-sit//v1/penta/art/policy-create-receipt' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'Accept: */*' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb' \
--header 'Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e' \
--data '{
    "policyNo": "IBER000300016567",

    "instrumentType": "DD",
    "instrumentBank": "ARB",
    "instrumentBankBranch": "CST ARB CUSTOMERS",
    "receiptCurrency": "SAR"
} '

Get All Policy for the User

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/list-policies?

Swagger Link

Returns all the policies user have based on user identification number.

Get All Policy for the User Workflow

Click here


Request Example

                                        
header:
    Accept: */*
    Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb
    Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e
Query Params: 
    idenCode:%3Cstring%3E
    idenNo:%3Cstring%3E    
                                        
                                      
                                          
curl --location 'https://apimarketplace-ext-sit-gw-svc-gw-gateway-traffic-cp4i-nprd.apimp-nprd-cl01-de8fb88b0db8c47d4745b3af8ac7158d-0000.eu-de.containers.appdomain.cloud/apimarketplace-sit/api-marketplace-sit//v1/penta/art/list-policies?idenCode=%3Cstring%3E&idenNo=%3Cstring%3E' \
--header 'Accept: */*' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb' \

Download Proposal Report

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/report-proposal

Swagger Link

Provides the data required from ISF to print the Proposal form. This API needs to be called to download updated proposal.

Request Example

                                                    
header:
    Accept: application/json
    x-request-id: a2a8a8dd-dca0-4626-9ea6-b372bf5aa143
    Authorization: ••••••
data {
    "transactionId": "98765433",
    "quotationNo": "API26419",
    "planType": "EDU",
    "language": "en"
} 
                                                    
                                                  
                                                      
curl --location 'https://test.api-marketplace.alrajhibank.com.sa/apimarketplace-sit/sandbox-inbound/v1/penta/art/report-proposal' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'x-request-id: a2a8a8dd-dca0-4626-9ea6-b372bf5aa143' \
--header 'Authorization: ••••••' \
--data '{
    "transactionId": "98765433",
    "quotationNo": "API26419",
    "planType": "EDU",
    "language": "en"
}'

Download Illustration Report

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/report-illustration

Swagger Link

Provides the data required to print the illustration report, This API needs to be called after initial proposal creation.

Request Example

                                                        
header:
    Accept: application/json
    x-request-id: bd4a11e8-06d9-4cab-8f03-7195d02a4234
    Authorization: ••••••
data {
    "transactionId": "98765433",
    "quotationNo": "API26419",
    "planType": "EDU",
    "language": "en"
}   
                                                        
                                                      
                                                          
curl --location 'https://test.api-marketplace.alrajhibank.com.sa/apimarketplace-sit/sandbox-inbound/v1/penta/art/report-illustration' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'x-request-id: bd4a11e8-06d9-4cab-8f03-7195d02a4234' \
--header 'Authorization: ••••••' \
--data '{
    "transactionId": "98765434",
    "quotationNo": "API26656",
    "planType": "EDU",
    "language": "en"
}'

Get Policy Number

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/policy-number/{quotationNo}?

Swagger Link

Get Policy number based on the quotation number.

Get Policy Number Workflow

Click here


Request Example

                                                            
header:
    Accept: */*
    Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb
    Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e
Query Params: 
    transactionId:1133   
                                                            
                                                          
                                                              
curl --location 'https://apimarketplace-ext-sit-gw-svc-gw-gateway-traffic-cp4i-nprd.apimp-nprd-cl01-de8fb88b0db8c47d4745b3af8ac7158d-0000.eu-de.containers.appdomain.cloud/apimarketplace-sit/api-marketplace-sit//v1/penta/art/policy-number/1233?transactionId=1133' \

Download Policy Report

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/proposal-policy

Swagger Link

Provides the data required to download policy report.

Request Example 

                                                                
header:
    Accept: application/json
    x-request-id: 8f4e0c07-cb6a-4dc8-8ecd-75ec1588be33
    Authorization: ••••••
data {
    "transactionId": "API26653",
    "policyNo": "IBER000300015080",
    "language": "en"
}
                                                                
                                                              
                                                                  
curl --location 'https://test.api-marketplace.alrajhibank.com.sa/apimarketplace-sit/sandbox-inbound/v1/penta/art/proposal-policy' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'x-request-id: 8f4e0c07-cb6a-4dc8-8ecd-75ec1588be33' \
--header 'Authorization: ••••••' \
--data '{
    "transactionId": "API26653",
    "policyNo": "IBER000300015080",
    "language": "en"
}'

    Response

200 401 403 404 405 406 429 500 

                                                        
{
    "status": "Success",

Get debiting day

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/debitday?

Swagger Link

Returns debiting day for user.

Request Example

                                                                    
header:
    Accept: application/json
    Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb
    Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e
Query Params: 
    transactionId:876534   
                                                                    
                                                                  
                                                                      
curl --location 'https://apimarketplace-ext-sit-gw-svc-gw-gateway-traffic-cp4i-nprd.apimp-nprd-cl01-de8fb88b0db8c47d4745b3af8ac7158d-0000.eu-de.containers.appdomain.cloud/apimarketplace-sit/api-marketplace-sit//v1/penta/art/debitday?transactionId=876534' \
--header 'Accept: application/json' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb' \
--header 'Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e'

Retrieve Customer Data

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/customer-data-retrieve?

Swagger Link

It is used to retrieve customer data from ELM, Saudi post and Gosi. These data needed for proposal creation.

Request Example

                                                                        
header:
    Accept: application/json
    Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb
    Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e
Query Params: 
    transactionId:876534
    idenType:SI
    idenNo:1062126535
    dateOfBirth:10-1988 
                                                                        
                                                                      
                                                                          
curl --location 'https://apimarketplace-ext-sit-gw-svc-gw-gateway-traffic-cp4i-nprd.apimp-nprd-cl01-de8fb88b0db8c47d4745b3af8ac7158d-0000.eu-de.containers.appdomain.cloud/apimarketplace-sit/api-marketplace-sit//v1/penta/art/customer-data-retrieve?transactionId=876534&idenType=SI&idenNo=1062126535&dateOfBirth=10-1988' \
--header 'Accept: application/json' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb' \
--header 'Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e'

Update Proposal or Quotation

Endpoint: /v1/penta/art/proposals-update/{quotation-no}

Swagger Link

It is used to update quotation. Once user enters all the questions (health, pregnancy or health related questions), fund details and existing policies, update quotation needs to be called.

Request Example

                                                                            
header:
    Content-Type: application/json
    Accept: application/json
        Authorization: Bearer AAIgZDJiY2YwZTcyZDc5YzFkZTFkMjAyMTQxY2RlZmI1ODG4MoMFAF2gMPl1rNcI2QniwBPZjLEC_gf_S0VEwBnARkJ0DlWoOxy7PRPakFSeb1goCG8lYPuP5O_yjOvKW5j1dNleGgOtiCD9gADoEQZrYLRGvcEWOtiLhDG393EKof5n-4w2DbVORXFIpGJJ74Vb
        Cookie: d70c4b71d548cdbd2b531dc96408ca96=fb0666a24d526b85c91e22d7a5cd873e
Request Body:
        data {
    "uwQuestions": [
        {
            "questionCode": "UW048",
            "reply": "n",
            "replyDetails": "2022-08-28"
        },
        {
            "questionCode": "UW030",
            "reply": "n"
        }
    ],
    "fundDetails": [
        {
            "fundCode": "CS001",
            "fundPercentage": "45"
        },
        {
            "fundCode": "BF001",
            "fundPercentage": "55"
        },
        {
            "fundCode": "AM001",
            "fundPercentage": "0"
        }
